- **start-cr**: Explicit-invocation orchestrator that runs the review lenses (comments, readability & tests, naming & module, objects & patterns, simplicity & types, correctness, security, coherence, assumptions, performance, spec, hookify) in parallel over a change and merges them into one per-file report. Lenses are gated by the input, never by the user: correctness, security, coherence and assumptions are always on, performance runs only when executable source files are in scope, spec only when a spec file is named — by `--spec &lt;path&gt;`, or by the user accepting the one the diff itself carries — and hookif… ([grixu/cc-toolkit](https://github.com/grixu/cc-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-review/commands/start-cr.md) · ★10)

- **stash**: Shelve uncommitted changes and restore them later. With no subcommand, `grit stash` saves local changes (same as `grit stash push`), resets the working tree and index to `HEAD`, and records a stash entry compatible with Git's `refs/stash` format. Skip-worktree paths are not stashed or reset on push, and the bit stays set through pop, matching Git. Unreadable worktree directories produce a warning ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/stash.md) · ★598)

- **stash**: Save dirty working-tree + index changes onto a stack and restore them later — faithful to `git stash`. Nested namespace: `repo.stash.{push,list,apply,pop,drop}`. The stack is the `refs/stash` reflog (`stash@{0}` is newest); each entry is a `WIP` commit whose parents are the base commit, the index commit, and (whenever `includeUntracked` is set) an untracked-files commit.
